# GHC 9.12.2 compatibility issues



## User



I have been working on a pure ts haskell to ts compiler based on ghc's js compiler 

in the early stages I though I could loosely base it on various compilers in @references but to get references/miso and @references/miso-lynx to even lauch, not only did it need to be exactly how ghc compiles it but it needed to be based on the exact version of ghc references/miso uses GHC 9.12.2 the problem is the current code does not match GHC 9.12.2 enough to run the app properly and there are may bug, swipes take 2 seconds to fire, taps completely miss unless you press button repeatedly animations are choppy, the list goes on but the root cause poorly matching GHC 9.12.2 in GHCTS We need to fix this 





The lynx bundle server is already running:



adb reverse tcp:8080 tcp:8080

adb shell am force-stop com.lynx.explorer && adb shell am start 

-n com.lynx.explorer/.LynxViewShellActivity 

--es lynx_initial_url [http://localhost:8080/counter/main.lynx.bundle](http://localhost:8080/counter/main.lynx.bundle)

{"plan":"# Match GHC 9.12.2 enough for Miso/Lynx to feel native\n\nGHCTS already has a real StgToJS port under [`src/stg-js/`](src/stg-js/) and ships GHC’s own `rts/js/*.js` via [`src/stg-js/rts-static.ts`](src/stg-js/rts-static.ts). The Lynx apps launch, but they are not running GHC 9.12.2’s contract. The reported bugs (missed taps, ~2s swipes, choppy frames) sit on the **event / SPT / apply** path, not on “needs a different compiler.”\n\nDo **not** keep patching [`references/miso`](references/miso). Revert compiler workarounds there once the backend matches GHC. Compare against the GHC oracle already served as `/counter-ghc/main.lynx.bundle` in [`scripts/host-lynx.mjs`](scripts/host-lynx.mjs).\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n  lynxEvent[Lynx event] --> mtsRoute[MTS routeEvent]\n  mtsRoute -->|\"staticKey lookup\"| spt[unsafeLookupStaticPtr]\n  spt -->|miss| droppedTap[tap dropped]\n  spt -->|hit| handler[Haskell handler]\n  handler --> callback[h$runSync / h$forkThread]\n  callback --> apply[h$ap_N_M_fast or h$ap_gen_fast]\n  apply --> vdomOrPaint[BTS patch or MTS PAPI]\n```\n\n## What is actually wrong\n\nGHC’s JavaScript backend is not “generate some lazy JS.” StgToJS reads **Core facts** (arity, `isStrictId`, `isStrictType`, tag signatures, `idDetails`) and then emits a specific apply/FFI/SPT shape. GHCTS ports the emitter but often answers those facts `false` / `undefined`, or skips the GHC hook entirely.\n\nHighest-impact mismatches for this app:\n\n1. **Static pointer table is not initialized the GHC way.** [`src/stg-js/codegen.ts`](src/stg-js/codegen.ts) drops `initStaticPtrs` (`\"Not modelled, and dropped\"`). GHC pushes SPT inserts onto `h$initStatic` and `h$runInitStatic` runs them **before** `main` on **every** JS engine. GHCTS instead chains `sptRegisterThen` in front of `main` in [`src/elaborate.ts`](src/elaborate.ts). On Lynx the MTS looks up `static (…)` keys to dispatch taps (`unsafeLookupStaticPtr` in Miso). If MTS has not run that `main` wrapper, or events race it, taps miss unless you hammer the button.\n\n2. **FFI callbacks were rewritten instead of using GHC’s.** [`src/library.ts`](src/library.ts) `GHC.JS.Foreign.Callback` inlines `h$c2(h$ap1_e, g, x)` and **skips `MK_JSVAL`**, because `MK_JSVAL` boxes with `h$ghczminternalZCGHCziInternalziJSziPrimziJSVal_con_e` ([`src/stg-js/rts-static.ts`](src/stg-js/rts-static.ts)). GHC uses `h$makeCallback` / `h$makeCallbackApply` from [`references/ghc/rts/js/mem.js`](references/ghc/rts/js/mem.js). Miso event handlers are `asyncCallback2`; MTS `eachFrame` is `syncCallback1`. Wrong boxing or async vs sync here is delayed swipes and dropped events.\n\n3. **`genApp` does not take GHC’s branches.** GHC [`Apply.hs`](references/ghc/compiler/GHC/StgToJS/Apply.hs) has evaluated/unboxed/newtype/oversaturated/saturated-global paths. GHCTS [`src/stg-js/expr.ts`](src/stg-js/expr.ts) documents these as `CONSERVATIVE` and falls through to `jumpToFast`. The new “GHC” saturated jump is gated on `i.strict === true`, but [`src/core-to-stg.ts`](src/core-to-stg.ts) only sets `strict` for native prim workers — so almost every call still pays `h$ap_N_M_fast` / `h$ap_gen_fast`. `ctxIsEvaluated` is hard-coded `false` in [`src/stg-js/expr-ctx.ts`](src/stg-js/expr-ctx.ts). `isInlineApp` always returns `false`.\n\n4. **Core pipeline is a stub of GHC 9.12.2.** GHC runs Simplifier, Specialise, DmdAnal, WorkWrap, CPR, CorePrep, InferTags, then CoreToStg. GHCTS runs a tiny inline pass + demand approximation ([`src/core-opt/`](src/core-opt/)), then ANF. That is why loops and event decode allocate thunks GHC unboxes away — choppy `eachFrame` / late `touchmove`.\n\nWIP already in the tree (`src/stg-js/expr.ts`, `src/core-to-stg.ts`, `src/stg.ts`, `optimization: \"ghc\"`) is the right direction. Finish it as GHC, not as a third calling convention.\n\n## Phase 0 — measure against the 9.12.2 oracle (before more codegen)\n\nUse tools that already exist. Do not guess.\n\n- [`scripts/oracle-diff.mjs`](scripts/oracle-diff.mjs) / [`test/compiler.test.mjs`](test/compiler.test.mjs): RTS symbol coverage vs `references/miso-lynx/oracle-jsexe/rts.js`.\n- [`scripts/codegen-corpus.mjs`](scripts/codegen-corpus.mjs) + [`scripts/stg-diff.mjs`](scripts/stg-diff.mjs): which STG facts and `h$` bodies still diverge (arity, thunk vs fun, first expression).\n- [`scripts/runtime-report.mjs`](scripts/runtime-report.mjs) on **both** `references/miso-lynx/examples/dist/jsexe/all.js` (ghcts) and the GHC counter `out.js`: `h$ap_gen_fast` vs specialized apply, `h$e` enters, update frames, `fromJSVal`.\n- [`scripts/lynx-run.mjs`](scripts/lynx-run.mjs) `--tree --tap …` to reproduce missed taps without the device.\n- Device A/B: current URL vs `http://localhost:8080/counter-ghc/main.lynx.bundle`. Same for misogram if swipe/animation is that app.\n\nRecord the numbers. Phase 1–3 must move those counters toward the GHC bundle, and taps must fire in `lynx-run` on the first dispatch.\n\n## Phase 1 — SPT the way GHC does (missed taps)\n\nWire [`src/stg-js/static-ptr.ts`](src/stg-js/static-ptr.ts) `initStaticPtrs` into `generateGlobalBlock` exactly as GHC’s `stgToJS` does: push `h$hs_spt_insert(k1,k2,k3,k4,ref)` thunks onto `h$initStatic` so `h$runInitStatic` fills the table on **both** Lynx threads before any event.\n\n- Collect `SptEntry` from `StaticPtr` constructor bindings (linker already special-cases them via `hasExport` in [`src/stg-js/utils.ts`](src/stg-js/utils.ts)).\n- Keep fingerprints as the four 32-bit MD5 words GHC’s `StgToJS.StaticPtr` passes; MTS/BTS of one bundle must agree. Do not require GHC-identical keys unless we also emit GHC’s tidy names.\n- Once init runs at RTS startup, remove the `sptRegisterThen` `main` wrapper if it is redundant (or keep it only if a test shows a second registration is required).\n\nAdd a unit test: compile `static (id :: a -> a)`, run `h$runInitStatic`, then `unsafeLookupStaticPtr` returns the same closure.\n\n## Phase 2 — GHC callback / JSVal ABI (taps + swipes)\n\nStop reimplementing callbacks.\n\n- Emit the JSVal data constructor **under GHC’s z-encoded name** `h$ghczminternalZCGHCziInternalziJSziPrimziJSVal_con_e` (already in [`src/stg-js/symbols.ts`](src/stg-js/symbols.ts)), or alias GHCTS’s `JSVal` constructor to that symbol so `MK_JSVAL` produces a heap object Haskell `case JSVal x` actually matches.\n- Replace the custom `jsCallback*` / `jsSyncCallback*` strings in [`src/library.ts`](src/library.ts) with GHC 9.12.2’s imports from [`references/ghc/libraries/ghc-internal/src/GHC/Internal/JS/Foreign/Callback.hs`](references/ghc/libraries/ghc-internal/src/GHC/Internal/JS/Foreign/Callback.hs): `h$makeCallback(h$runSync, …)`, `h$makeCallbackApply`, `h$run`. Keep the “name `h$runSync` directly, not `globalThis['h$runSync']`” lesson — that part is correct for bundled Lynx JS.\n- Confirm Miso’s `asyncCallback2` (vdom events) and `syncCallback1` (`eachFrame`) both complete: sync must finish inside `h$runSync` / `h$runThreadSlice` without leaving the MTS event handler; async must schedule like GHC, not stall the UI thread for seconds.\n\n## Phase 3 — make `genApp` take GHC’s branches\n\nPopulate the Binder fields StgToJS already reads, then copy the missing `Apply.hs` cases into [`src/stg-js/expr.ts`](src/stg-js/expr.ts).\n\nFacts (set in Core→STG / desugar, not guessed in codegen):\n\n- `funRepArity` on **every** function RHS (lambda count), not only native workers.\n- `isStrictId` stand-in: unlifted / known-evaluated / **top-level Fun with arity > 0** (GHC’s `isStrictType || isStrUsedDmd`; functions used as functions are not thunks). Today `strict` is almost never set, so the saturated jump never fires.\n- `mightBeFun`: False only for known algebraic data (already the documented rule).\n- `evaluated`: True after a `case` binder and for saturated constructor results (cheap InferTags). Then `ctxIsEvaluated` can stop returning False forever.\n\nCodegen (match [`Apply.hs` order](references/ghc/compiler/GHC/StgToJS/Apply.hs)):\n\n- No-arg strict / unboxed field return (`ExprInline`).\n- Unboxable `Int`/`Double` payload when evaluated.\n- Newtype wrapper.\n- Saturated **global** `return entry` (`jumpToII`).\n- **Oversaturated**: `pushCont` extra args, then jump (currently missing entirely).\n- Else `jumpToFast` unchanged.\n\nKeep `optimization: \"none\"` as the conservative apply path for oracle diffs. Default stays `\"ghc\"`. Do not expand `\"native\"` until the GHC path is correct.\n\nTests: extend [`test/ghc-js.test.mjs`](test/ghc-js.test.mjs) so `add x x` emits `return h$…_e` (or GHC’s equivalent), not only `h$ap_2_2_fast`. Corpus `saturated` / `partial` / `apply` in [`scripts/codegen-corpus.mjs`](scripts/codegen-corpus.mjs) should move toward byte match.\n\n## Phase 4 — CorePrep / demand, only what StgToJS pays for\n\nAfter Phase 3, if `runtime-report` still shows GHC allocating far fewer thunks:\n\n- Widen [`src/core-opt/strictness.ts`](src/core-opt/strictness.ts) so **callers** of known-strict functions get `case` (already the intent) and so `isStrictId` on those functions is true.\n- Worker/wrapper for `Int`/`Double` is the next GHC pass (`WorkWrap.hs`). Only add it if Phase 3 + demand still leaves `eachFrame` over budget versus the GHC bundle. Do not invent a third native-region ABI.\n\n## Phase 5 — verify on device; unhack Miso\n\nRebuild: `npm run build:lynx`. Reload Lynx Explorer on the existing `adb reverse` / `8080` server.\n\nDone when, versus `/counter-ghc` (and GHC misogram if present):\n\n- Tap fires on the first press.\n- Swipe tracking is immediate (not ~2s late).\n- `eachFrame` / list scroll stays smooth.\n- `lynx-run --tap` dispatches first try.\n- `runtime-report` apply/enter/update counts are in the same order of magnitude as the GHC `all.js`.\n\nThen revert [`references/miso`](references/miso) JS/TS/HS edits unless a change is a real upstream Miso bug independent of GHCTS.\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- Full GHC simplifier / specialiser / byte-identical `out.js` for the whole Miso graph.\n- Matching other compilers under `references/` (GHCJS, MicroHs, aihc).\n- Further Miso native workarounds as a substitute for the backend.\n","todos":[{"id":"measure-oracle","content":"Baseline ghcts vs GHC 9.12.2 oracle: oracle-diff, codegen-corpus, stg-diff, runtime-report, lynx-run taps","status":"TODO_STATUS_PENDING","createdAt":"1788195875348","updatedAt":"1788195875348","dependencies":[]},{"id":"spt-init","content":"Call initStaticPtrs from generateGlobalBlock so h$runInitStatic fills SPT on both Lynx threads before main","status":"TODO_STATUS_PENDING","createdAt":"1788195875348","updatedAt":"1788195875348","dependencies":[]},{"id":"jsval-callbacks","content":"Alias JSVal to GHC z-name; # GHCTS Architecture Report



## What it is



**ghcts** is a TypeScript-native Haskell→JavaScript compiler that aims to reproduce **GHC 9.12.2’s JavaScript backend** (`GHC.StgToJS` / `GHC.JS`), not a bespoke JS runtime. Output is a real GHC-shaped `.jsexe` (`rts.js`, `lib.js`, `out.js`, `runmain.js`, `all.js`). The live benchmark is the reachable default-browser **Miso** graph (and Lynx-native variants).



---



## 1. Project structure



```

ghcts/

├── src/ # Compiler (TypeScript)

│ ├── build.ts # Full pipeline driver

│ ├── cli.ts / server.ts # `ghcts build|check|serve`

│ ├── parser.ts / lexer.ts / sugar.ts / modules.ts

│ ├── elaborate.ts / dictionary.ts / typecheck.ts

│ ├── desugar.ts → core.ts → core-to-stg.ts → stg.ts

│ ├── stg-js/ # Port of GHC.StgToJS (codegen + RTS)

│ ├── js/ # Port of GHC.JS (AST, printer, optimizer)

│ ├── linker/linker.ts # Assembly into .jsexe

│ ├── prelude.ts / library.ts # Wired-in boot libs

│ └── types.ts # GhctsConfig / Diagnostic

├── scripts/ # Diffs, runners, Lynx packaging

├── test/ # Node test suite

├── references/ # Submodules: miso, ghc, ghcjs, aihc, miso-lynx, misogram, …

└── package.json

```



### Key source files and roles



| Path | Role |

|------|------|

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/types.ts` | `GhctsConfig` (`sourceRoots`, `entry`, `cpp`, `flags`, `optimization`: `none` \| `ghc` \| `native`), diagnostics |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/build.ts` | **Pipeline**: parse → elaborate → desugar → Core opt → STG → StgToJS → link → emit `.jsexe`; CPP `GHCJS_BOTH`/`GHCJS_NEW`; `:Main` split; package `js-sources` |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg.ts` | STG IR (from `GHC/Stg/Syntax.hs`); saturated apps, free vars, update flags |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/core.ts` | Reduced Core; `Con`/`Op` always saturated |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/core-to-stg.ts` | Core→STG (ANF, free vars, update flags, native-eligibility seeds) |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-to-js.ts` | Re-exports `stg-js/*` |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/desugar.ts` | Surface AST → Core (expects dictionary-elaborated input) |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/elaborate.ts` | Imports, classes/instances → dictionaries; Prelude injection |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/codegen.ts` | `stgToJS` / `genUnits` / `renderModule` (GHC 9.12.2 CodeGen) |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/expr.ts` | Expression codegen (`genApp`, cases, etc.) |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/prim.ts` | Primops |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/apply.ts` / `rts.ts` | Apply helpers + generated RTS |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/rts-static.ts` | Verbatim GHC `js-sources` for `lib.js` |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/types.ts` | ClosureType/JSRep/CILayout; `optimization` modes |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/linker/linker.ts` | Link LinkableUnits → `.jsexe` |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/js/*` | GHC.JS AST, Ppr, Optimizer |

| `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/core-opt/` | `inline.ts`, `strictness.ts` (narrow Core→Core) |



**`src/stg-js/` modules** (1:1 with GHC): `apply`, `arg`, `closure`, `codegen`, `datacon`, `expr`, `expr-ctx`, `ffi`, `heap`, `ids`, `literal`, `monad`, `prim`, `regs`, `rts`, `rts-static`, `rts-types`, `sinker`, `stack`, `static-ptr`, `symbols`, `types`, `utils`.



---



## 2. README status & architecture (docs)



From `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/README.md`:



- Install: `npm install ghcts`; project needs `ghcts.json` (or a discoverable `.cabal`).

- Commands: `npx ghcts build|serve --project .`

- Emits inspectable TS/ESM-style pipeline output; Miso is compiled from checked-in Haskell (no fake counter / `Miso.*` replacement).

- Browser builds package Miso’s JS assets; Node `serve` for local testing.

- **Optimization**: default `ghc` (ABI-preserving); `none` = conservative path; `native` = experimental, falls back to GHC RTS convention.

- **Intentionally unsupported**: Hackage solving, Template Haskell execution, arbitrary C FFI, remaining GHC extension surface.

- Influences: miso, GHCJS, MicroHs, GHC, aihc (submodules under `references/`).



---



## 3. Compiler pipeline



```

Haskell sources

 │ loadProgram (import closure only; CPP: GHCJS_BOTH, GHCJS_NEW, + config.cpp)

 ▼

parse (lexer + layout + parser)

 ▼

elaborateProgram (imports, Prelude, class/instance → dictionaries)

 ▼

desugarModule (AST → Core)

 ▼

Core opt (if optimization ≠ "none"):

 inlineKnownCalls → forceStrictArgs

 ▼

coreToStg (ANF / free vars / UpdateFlag; optional nativeEligible)

 ▼

stgToJS / genUnits (sinker → LinkableUnits; GHC calling convention)

 ▼

ghcModules (split Main / :Main / RTS seats; shared Id cache)

 ▼

link → rts.js + lib.js + out.js + runmain.js

 + packageJs (dependency cabal js-sources, e.g. miso runtime)

 ▼

all.js = rts + lib + out + runmain → <outDir>/jsexe/

```



**Important design constraints** (from headers):



- Output must speak **GHC’s calling convention** (registers, `h$ap_*`, closures) because the RTS is GHC’s.

- STG invariants: saturated atomic args; free vars + update flags on every closure.

- Core drops Cast/Tick/Type/Coercion; constructors/primops are dedicated saturated nodes.



---



## 4. Known GHC compatibility issues (code/docs)



### Documented product gaps (README)

- Hackage solving, TH execution, arbitrary C FFI, leftover GHC extensions → diagnosed unsupported.



### Explicit divergences / FIXMEs in `src/`



| Location | Issue |

|----------|--------|

| `elaborate.ts` (~1938) | **GND / deriving newtype**: unwrap via `case` → **stricter than GHC** (forces args GHC might leave lazy) |

| `stg-js/expr.ts:490` | `fixme`: selector offset > 16 not configurable |

| `stg-js/expr.ts:774` | `fixme`: static refs should use newer dynamic computation; keeps safe **superset** (no types to drop unlifted) |

| `stg-js/expr.ts:1223` | `fixme`: `use = repeat True` cleanup |

| `stg-js/codegen.ts` | Not modelled: StaticPointers, ForeignStubs, cost centres, deps, `-ddump-js` |

| `stg.ts` / `core-to-stg.ts` | No full CorePrep; float-to-top only for always-sinkable cons; **SingleEntry never emitted** |

| `stg.ts` Binder | No Core Id/type; `mightBeFun` approximated; `undefined` = unknown |

| `build.ts` | Without `GHCJS_NEW`/`GHCJS_BOTH`, miso hits old `$r = $1` FFI **rejected by GHC 9.12** |

| `stg-js/ffi.ts` | Rejects GHCJS-style `$1`/`$r` holes; requires JS **function expression** (9.12) |

| `core.ts` | Intentional: Con/Op as nodes; binders shared with STG |

| `desugar.ts` header | Unsupported surface constructs → diagnostic + raising expr (header may lag what’s implemented; tests cover `do`, list comps, etc.) |

| `parser` / `ast` | `unsupported` decls (e.g. foreign export) → typecheck `E_UNSUPPORTED` |

| `stg-js/prim.ts` | Vectors unsupported on JS backend |



### Diff tooling (expected mismatches)

- `scripts/stg-diff.mjs`: demand/`cpr` signatures have **no full ghcts counterpart** yet.

- `scripts/codegen-corpus.mjs` / `oracle-diff.mjs`: measure byte/symbol parity vs real GHC 9.12.2 JS output.



---



## 5. What `test/ghc-js.test.mjs` tests



Focused on **GHC.JS** and a slice of codegen:



1. **Optimization / ABI** – known-arity call still uses `h$ap_2_2_fast` (strictness needed for true direct-entry; `none` vs default still match that pattern in this fixture).

2. **Ident / JStgMonad** – `name`, `newIdent`, `withTag`.

3. **Transform** – staged→final JS; JHash key sort.

4. **Ppr** – string escapes (incl. astral), negative ints/doubles, empty else/catch, sorted hashes.

5. **Optimizer** – `h$sp` fusion, `++i` / `+=`, Opt.Expr folds, ternary→`!!`, Opt.Simple dead decl float.

6. **Make** – `if_`/`eq_`/`app`, `jVar`/`jFunction`.



Broader coverage lives in **`test/compiler.test.mjs`** (~50+ tests): Cabal→config derivation, Core opts, parsing (operators, lenses, FFI, CPP, TH lexing, Miso QQ), dictionaries, **RTS symbol families**, and **behavioral differential vs** `references/miso-lynx/oracle-jsexe/rts.js` (GHC 9.12.2).



---



## 6. `scripts/runtime-report.mjs`



Static **bundle shape metrics** (no execution)—safe for browser/Lynx bundles:



- Counts: `h$` functions, `h$ap_gen_fast`, specialized `h$ap_N_M_fast`, `h$e(`, direct `return h$*_e`, update frames, closure allocs, marshal `fromJSVal`/`toJSVal`, FFI refs.

- Usage: `node scripts/runtime-report.mjs [--json] <bundle.js>` 

 Default: `references/misogram/dist/jsexe/out.js`.



---



## 7. `package.json` scripts & building Miso/Lynx



```json

"build": "tsc -p tsconfig.json",

"build:lynx": "npm run build && node scripts/build-lynx-bundles.mjs",

"build:bundles": "npm run build:lynx",

"test": "npm run build && node --test test/**/*.test.mjs",

"check": "tsc -p tsconfig.json --noEmit"

```



CLI (after `npm run build`): `node dist/cli.js build|check|serve --project <dir>`.



### Browser Miso (sample-app style)



```sh

# With ghcts.json, or derive from .cabal:

npx ghcts build --project references/miso/sample-app

# → <outDir>/jsexe/all.js

npx ghcts serve --project .

```



Config can be derived from an `executable` cabal stanza (`deriveCabalConfig`); test fixture: `test/fixtures/miso-counter/ghcts.json`.



### Lynx / native Miso



`npm run build:lynx` → `scripts/build-lynx-bundles.mjs`:



1. `node --stack-size=60000 dist/cli.js build --project references/miso-lynx/examples` 

 (`ghcts.json`: `NATIVE`/`PRODUCTION` cpp+flags, miso src + ffi)

2. `pack-lynx.mjs` → `main.lynx.bundle` (minify, rspeedy/`pluginReactLynx`)

3. Same for `references/misogram` (+ styles/assets)



Helpers:



| Script | Purpose |

|--------|---------|

| `host-lynx.mjs` | Serve Lynx bundles on LAN (+ optional GHC oracle routes) |

| `lynx-run.mjs` | Node stub of Lynx MTS/BTS dual-thread host |

| `hs-run.mjs` | Compile/run small HS end-to-end under trampoline RTS |

| `stg-run.mjs` | Hand-built STG codegen smoke |

| `oracle-diff.mjs` / `static-rts-diff.mjs` / `ghc-js-diff.mjs` / `codegen-corpus.mjs` / `stg-diff.mjs` | Parity vs GHC 9.12.2 |



---



## Architecture overview (one picture)



```

 ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐

 Haskell 2010 / │ Frontend (ghcts-owned) │

 Miso graph │ lexer → parser → elaborate → │

 │ desugar → Core → [inline/strict] │

 └──────────────┬──────────────────────┘

 │ STG

 ┌──────────────▼──────────────────────┐

 │ Backend (GHC 9.12.2 StgToJS port) │

 │ sinker → codegen → GHC.JS ppr/opt │

 │ + rts.ts + rts-static.js-sources │

 └──────────────┬──────────────────────┘

 │ LinkableUnits

 ┌──────────────▼──────────────────────┐

 │ Linker → .jsexe (+ miso js-sources)│

 │ optional pack-lynx / serve │

 └─────────────────────────────────────┘

```



**Bottom line:** ghcts is a front half that produces STG plus a faithful StgToJS/RTS/linker port targeting GHC 9.12.2 ABI. Success is measured by RTS/oracle parity and compiling the real Miso (browser + Lynx) graphs—not by inventing a separate Haskell-in-JS model.

</response>

title: Find GHC mismatch evidence

agent_id: 13bc106f-da98-4d01-9a93-b170a2e2c372

detail: <user_visible_high_level_summary>

Most likely UI lag comes from GHC-shaped apply/thunk conservatism (generic `h$ap_*`, no InferTags, always-updatable frames) plus event/FFI marshalling—not the unfinished direct-jump WIP. Also check miso’s `SystemInfo.osVersion` / `eventJSON` fixes, which can make swipes inert and drop Lynx event payloads.

</user_visible_high_level_summary>

<response>

## Verdict



UI lag is most consistent with **systematic “slower-correct” STG/JS lowering** (extra `h$ap_*` / thunk entries / update frames on every event), plus **event/FFI marshalling cost**. The current WIP “GHC mode direct jump” does **not** yet fix that for ordinary calls. Separately, **miso working-tree fixes** explain some swipe/miss symptoms that are **not** compiler ABI bugs.



---



## Ranked likely bugs (event lag / missed taps / choppy anim)



### 1. Saturated calls still go through `h$ap_*` (highest impact)



**Why it hurts UI:** Every Miso event → decode → update → VDOM walk is a flood of apps. One extra apply frame each call stacks into multi‑100ms–seconds of Haskell work on the JS thread.



**Evidence:**

- Direct jump requires `i.strict === true` **and** matching arity (`src/stg-js/expr.ts` ~365–386).

- `strict` is only set for a **narrow nativeEligible primop worker** (`src/core-to-stg.ts` ~114–140), not for normal exported functions.

- New test still expects `h$ap_2_2_fast` for `add`/`use` in **both** `ghc` and `none` modes (`test/ghc-js.test.mjs` ~14–29).

- Header documents intentional conservatism: extra indirection via `h$ap_N_M` / `h$ap_gen_fast` (`src/stg-js/expr.ts` ~8–13, ~308–321).



**Symptom fit:** 2s swipe response, missed taps under load, choppy frames.



---



### 2. `ctxIsEvaluated` always false (InferTags unported)



**Why:** Evaluated values are re-entered as possible thunks (`h$e` / `h$ap_0_0`).



**Evidence:** `src/stg-js/expr-ctx.ts` ~102–109 — “conservatively False… only ever costing a redundant entry.” Tied to missing `GHC.Stg.InferTags` (`src/stg-js/expr.ts` ~313–316).



---



### 3. Every updatable thunk gets a full update frame (no one-shot)



**Why:** Event/update thunks push update frames that GHC would often blackhole-only.



**Evidence:** `genUpdFrame` CONSERVATIVE (`src/stg-js/expr.ts` ~742–751); `Binder.oneShot` exists (`src/stg.ts` ~60–61) but is **never populated** in ghcts sources.



---



### 4. `isInlineApp` / foreign inline always conservative



**Why:** Extra return frames on apps and many FFI calls on the event path.



**Evidence:** `isInlineApp` always `false` (`src/stg-js/expr.ts` ~205–212); `isInlineExpr` foreign branch CONSERVATIVE (“one frame slower”, ~191–196).



---



### 5. Core demand analysis incomplete vs GHC 9.12.2 (partially fixed, still behind)



**Why:** Thunk alloc/blackhole/enter on hot loops and handlers; prior microbench was **2–6× slower** before Core-to-Core; still slower than GHC on loops.



**Evidence:**

- `forceStrictArgs` / `inlineKnownCalls` in `src/build.ts` ~350–359.

- Commit `f69d3864`: “Emitted code ran 2-6x slower…”; loop still `0.13s` vs GHC `0.08s`.

- `scripts/stg-diff.mjs`: GHC `str`/`cpr` have “no ghcts counterpart yet.”

- Strictness results are **not** wired into `Binder.strict` for `genApp` (only nativeEligible prim workers get `strict`).



---



### 6. Event callbacks = sync/async RTS entry via `h$ap1_e` + scheduler



**Why:** Miso registers `asyncCallback2` for DOM events and `syncCallback*` elsewhere. Heavy Haskell in a sync callback blocks the UI thread; async depends on `h$mainLoop` + `requestAnimationFrame` yield (`GHCJS_BUSY_YIELD` default **500ms** in GHC RTS).



**Evidence:**

- Callback wrappers: `src/library.ts` ~1757–1825 (`h$forkThread` / `h$runSync` + `h$c2(h$ap1_e, …)`).

- Miso: `references/miso/src/Miso/Event.hs` ~208 (`asyncCallback2`), ~257+ (`syncCallback`).

- RTS yield: `references/ghc/rts/js/thread.js` ~721–776.



**Symptom fit:** Choppy animation (long slices before rAF), taps dropped while Haskell holds the thread.



---



### 7. JSVal / `fromJSVal` marshalling on every event payload



**Why:** Decode cost + historical GHCJS mismatch bugs surface as “press repeatedly” / silent fails.



**Evidence:**

- Commit `f2dadfed`: unchecked reads rejected non-matching types; miso JSON bool-as-Int broke.

- `src/library.ts` ~2887–2898 (`fromJSValUnchecked` / `describeJSVal` with `JSON.stringify`).

- `scripts/runtime-report.mjs` explicitly counts `fromJSVal` / `toJSVal` / FFI / applies as lag proxies.



---



### 8. Miso working-tree bugs that *look* like compiler lag (confirm first)



These are **in the dirty miso submodule**, not STG lowering:



| Issue | Evidence | Symptom |

|--------|----------|---------|

| Required `osVersion` breaks `SystemInfo` decode → `screenWidthPx = 0` | `references/miso/src/Miso/Native/MainThread.hs` diff (~193–201): “what made the **swiper inert**” | Swipe does nothing / feels broken |

| `eventJSON` dropped nested plain objects | `references/miso/ts/miso/event.ts` (~236–252): Lynx `detail` / animation / layout decoders saw nothing | Missed animation/layout events |



Treat these as **orthogonal** to “poor GHC 9.12.2 match,” but they must be ruled out before blaming codegen.



---



## Current working-tree changes (compiler)



| File | Intent |

|------|--------|

| `src/stg.ts` / `src/types.ts` | `funRepArity`, `strict`, `oneShot`, `nativeEligible`, `optimization` |

| `src/core-to-stg.ts` | Record arity; set `strict` only for closed prim workers |

| `src/stg-js/expr.ts` | ABI-safe saturated direct jump (gated on `strict`+`exported`) |

| `src/stg-js/types.ts` | Default `optimization: "ghc"` |

| `README.md` | Documents `none` / `ghc` / `native` |

| `test/ghc-js.test.mjs` | Shows direct jump **does not** fire for normal `add` |

| `scripts/runtime-report.mjs` | Static counters: `h$ap_*`, enters, updates, marshal, FFI |



**Bottom line:** WIP is scaffolding for GHC-matching apply; it is **not yet effective** on the call patterns that drive UI.



---



## Recommended investigation path



1. **Confirm miso payload bugs on device** 

 Rebuild with `MainThread.hs` `osVersion :: Maybe` + `eventJSON` depth recursion; retest swipe/tap. If swipe was “inert,” that was geometry/decode, not STG.



2. **Measure apply shape on the real Lynx/misogram bundle** 

 `node scripts/runtime-report.mjs <bundle.js>` — baseline `genericApplies` / `specializedApplies` / `directEntryReturns` / marshal / FFI. After any apply fix, those should move.



3. **Wire demand → `Binder.strict` (or GHC’s real `isStrictId` guard)** 

 Today Core `forceStrictArgs` rewrites calls, but codegen direct-jump still needs `i.strict`. Either:

 - propagate arg-strictness / known-fun facts onto binders used at call sites, or 

 - match GHC: saturated jump when arity matches **without** inventing a dead `strict` bit for prim-only workers.



4. **Diff hot modules vs GHC 9.12.2** 

 `scripts/ghc-js-diff.mjs` / `scripts/stg-diff.mjs` / `scripts/bench-vs-ghc.mjs` on a tiny event-handler-shaped program (decode + map + fold), not only `sumTo`.



5. **Instrument event path latency** 

 Time: JS listener → callback → `fromJSVal` → update → diff → paint. If sync callbacks dominate, check `syncCallback` vs `asyncCallback2` and whether `h$runSync` runs long slices without yielding.



6. **Port or approximate InferTags + one-shot** 

 After apply is fixed: `ctxIsEvaluated` and `genUpdFrame` one-shot — next largest “extra entry / update frame” tax on animation.



7. **Only then** expand `native` / direct-entry beyond ABI-safe cases; keep fallbacks measured with `runtime-report.mjs`.



---



**Most probable root cause of multi-second / missed-event UX:** still-generic apply + redundant thunk entry/update on a hot event/VDOM path (items 1–3), amplified by sync/async callback + JSVal decode (6–7). Fix miso SystemInfo/`eventJSON` in parallel so swipe “inertness” is not mistaken for STG mismatch.

</response>

detail: <user_visible_high_level_summary>

GHCTS ports GHC 9.12.2’s StgToJS/RTS closely for symbols (RTS families match; lib.js ~98% identical), but module codegen is only 9/23 byte-identical, STG lacks types/tag-sigs/CPR, and several StgToJS pieces (Object/Deps/Profiling wiring) are dropped or approximated. Miso builds JS via bun from `ts/`; miso-lynx is the Lynx/native consumer with the GHC oracle `.jsexe`.

<response>

## Verdict



GHCTS deliberately aims at **GHC 9.12.2’s JavaScript backend** (`references/ghc` @ `ghc-9.12.2-release`), not classic GHCJS. The **generated RTS symbol surface matches** the oracle; **module codegen does not** (live corpus: **9/23** byte-identical, **12/23** after local-name masking). Upstream of StgToJS (Core prep, demand/CPR/tag sigs, typed binders) is where most divergence lives.



---



## 1. `references/miso`



**Structure**

- Haskell: `src/Miso/` (+ `Native/` for Lynx dual-thread)

- TS → JS: `ts/` → `js/` via **bun** (`package.json`: `build` / `prod` / `build-native` / `prod-native`)

- Cabal flags pick `js-sources`: `miso.js` / `miso.prod.js` / `miso-native.js` / `miso-native.prod.js`

- CPP: `arch(javascript)` → `GHCJS_NEW` + `GHCJS_BOTH` (ghcts mirrors this in `src/build.ts`)



**GHC version (docs/build)**

- Install docs show **GHC 9.12.2** JS (`javascript-ghcjs`) and WASM (`9.12.2.20250327`)

- Submodule tip: `ad44bf85` (miso 1.12.0+)



**How JS is generated**

1. **App/Haskell** → GHC JS backend → `.jsexe` (`rts.js`, `lib.js`, `out.js`, `runmain.js`)

2. **VDOM/runtime** → bun bundles `ts/index.ts` / `ts/miso-native.ts` into checked-in `js/*.js` (linked as Cabal `js-sources`, not produced by GHC)



---



## 2. `references/miso-lynx`



- Standalone Lynx/mobile demo repo; README: **upstreamed into miso** (`Miso.Native`)

- Pins miso via `source-repository-package`; `package miso` flags: `+native`

- Counter uses `StaticPointers`, `Miso.Native`, Lynx dual-thread

- **`oracle-jsexe/`**: real GHC 9.12.2 output for the counter (golden for RTS/lib diffs)

- **Not** a git submodule in `.gitmodules` (present on disk; miso/ghc/ghcjs/aihc are)



---



## 3. GHC source references



| Path | Role |

|------|------|

| `references/ghc` | Full GHC **9.12.2**; `compiler/GHC/StgToJS/*`, `compiler/GHC/JS/*`, `rts/js/*` |

| `references/ghcjs` | Classic GHCJS (**ghc-8.10** branch) — historical FFI/CPP inspiration |

| Nix path in scripts | `javascript-unknown-ghcjs-ghc-…-9.12.2` (miso-lynx flake) |



GHCTS ports map 1:1 under `src/stg-js/` ↔ `GHC/StgToJS`, `src/js/` ↔ `GHC/JS`.



**Missing / incomplete vs GHC StgToJS**

- No real `Object.hs` / `Deps.hs` object format (in-memory `LinkableUnit`s instead)

- `Profiling.hs` not ported (stubs / `ifProfiling` → mempty)

- `StaticPtr` module exists but codegen historically dropped SPT wiring; STG diff still shows StaticPtr shape mismatches

- Linker skips emcc/clibs, HTML runner, `out.frefs.*` (oracle `NOTES.txt` notes clibs omitted)



---



## 4. GHC 9.12.2 JS backend vs this project



**GHC pipeline:** Core → CorePrep → CoreToStg → **StgToJS** → link `.jsexe`



**GHCTS pipeline:** parse → elaborate → desugar → (partial Core opts) → `core-to-stg` → StgToJS port → linker



| Layer | GHC | GHCTS |

|-------|-----|-------|

| RTS `rts.js` | `jsOptimize (jStgStatToJS <$> rts cfg)` | `src/stg-js/rts.ts` — families **247/247** `h$ppN`, etc. match oracle |

| Static RTS `lib.js` | CPP’d `rts/js/*` + `ghc-internal/jsbits` | `rts-static.ts` — **608/618** shared bodies identical; oracle has **~102** extra (miso/other pkg JS) |

| Module `out.js` | Full Core+STG fidelity | **9/23** byte-identical corpus |

| AST | `GHC.JS` | Faithful port in `src/js/` + `ghc-js.ts`; older ad-hoc AST still noted as separate |



---



## 5. RTS: GHC vs GHCTS



**GHC**

- Hand-written: `references/ghc/rts/js/{rts,apply,gc,thread,…}.js`

- Generated half: `GHC.StgToJS.Rts` + `Apply` → `rts.js`

- Linked into `lib.js` (static) + `rts.js` (generated)



**GHCTS**

- Generated: `src/stg-js/rts.ts`, `apply.ts`

- Static: verbatim GHC sources via `rts-static.ts` + CPP (`genCommonCppDefs`)

- Diff harnesses: `scripts/oracle-diff.mjs`, `static-rts-diff.mjs`



**Practical RTS gap:** symbol families OK; remaining diffs are whitespace/CPP TRACE noise and **package JS not in ghcts’s static list** (miso helpers in oracle `lib.js`).



---



## 6. Recent git history (ghcts)



Focus of recent commits: STG differential vs GHC dumps, demand analysis, StaticPointers, Core inlining/strictness, FFI/marshal GHCJS-compat, miso/browser sample “compiles”.



Differential tooling: `stg-diff.mjs`, `ghc-js-diff.mjs`, `codegen-corpus.mjs`, `miso-gap.mjs`, `runtime-report.mjs`.



---



## 7. Other inspiration under `references/`



| Ref | Role (per README / layout) |

|-----|----------------------------|

| **miso** | Benchmark app + packaged JS assets |

| **ghcjs** | Lazy eval, `foreign import javascript`, CPP (`GHCJS_*`) |

| **ghc** | Wired-in libs, Core/STG/JS backend |

| **aihc** | Phased compiler / layout parsing patterns |

| **MicroHs** | Minimal combinator/JS runtime (inspiration only; not submodule) |

| **miso-lynx** | Native/Lynx + oracle `.jsexe` |

| **misogram**, **video** | Extra fixtures |



---



## Specific known mismatches (evidence)



1. **Codegen corpus: 9/23 byte-identical** — fails on arith, partial apply, let, recursion, cmp, lambda, guards, CAF thunks; several only differ by local symbol rename.

2. **STG facts: 3/11 mismatched** — StaticPtr as `con` vs `thunk`/`call:$static0`; `add` head `call:$fNumInt_$c` vs `case`; no **str/cpr** analyses in ghcts (`stg-diff.mjs`).

3. **No Core types on binders** — `utils.ts` / `expr.ts`: Utils pieces that need types omitted; `resultSize` can’t do GHC’s typed computation; `ctxIsEvaluated` always `false` (no InferTags) → redundant `h$e`.

4. **Newtype:** never erased (`desugar.ts` / `elaborate.ts`) — unlike GHC; unwrap via `case` → stricter.

5. **FFI:** GHC 9.12 expression-style only; GHCJS `$1`/`$r` patterns rejected (`ffi.ts`).

6. **Regs helpers:** some APIs marked “10.1 only” / deprecated vs 9.12.2 (`regs.ts`).

7. **Profiling / Object / Deps / SPT wiring** dropped or stubbed (`codegen.ts`, `rts.ts`).

8. **Core opts incomplete:** worker/wrapper, full demand/CPR not there; `strictness.ts` is an approximation checked against GHC signatures.

9. **Two JS ASTs:** faithful `GHC.JS` port vs older pipeline AST — migration called out as follow-up (`ghc-js.ts`).

10. **ghcjs submodule is 8.10-era** — useful for history, not the 9.12.2 backend under test.



**What *does* match closely:** generated RTS entry families vs `oracle-jsexe/rts.js`; behavioral RTS probes in `test/compiler.test.mjs`; ~98% of shared static RTS function bodies.

</response>

GHCJS RTS `GHCJS_BUSY_YIELD` defaults to **500ms** (`references/ghc/rts/js/thread.js`); long `h$runSync` slices plus that yield window also chop animation. {"path":"/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/expr-ctx.ts","streamContent":"/** Predicate: do we know for sure that the given Id is evaluated?\n *\n * GHC answers this from the tag signature InferTags attached to the Id\n * (`idTagSig_maybe`, plus a JoinId veto on `idDetails`). ghcts records the\n * same fact on `Binder.evaluated` after a `case` (the scrutinee is WHNF)\n * and on `Binder.strict` for function closures, which are never thunks. */\nexport function ctxIsEvaluated(i: Binder): boolean {\n  if (i.join) return false;\n  return i.evaluated === true || i.strict === true;\n}"}

{"path":"/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/utils.ts","streamContent":"/** True when the binding is a `StaticPtr` constructor application, which the\n * linker must export. GHC compares against `staticPtrDataConKey`. A `static e`\n * thunk that still carries its fingerprint is the same root. */\nexport function hasExport(bnd: StgBinding): boolean {\n  const isExportedBind = (binder: Binder, rhs: StgRhs): boolean =>\n    binder.sptFingerprint !== undefined || (rhs.tag === \"StgRhsCon\" && rhs.con.name === \"StaticPtr\");\n  return bnd.tag === \"StgNonRec\"\n    ? isExportedBind(bnd.binder, bnd.rhs)\n    : bnd.pairs.some(([b, r]) => isExportedBind(b, r));\n}"}

{"path":"/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/codegen.ts","streamContent":"/** Generate the ingredients for the linkable units for this module */\nexport function genUnits(gs: GenState, m: Module, ss: StgBinding[]): LinkableUnit[] {\n  const gbl = generateGlobalBlock(gs, m, sptEntriesOf(ss));\n  const exports = generateExportsBlock(m);\n  const others: LinkableUnit[] = [];\n  for (const x of ss) {\n    const mlu = generateBlock(gs, x);\n    if (mlu !== undefined) others.push(mlu);\n  }\n  return [gbl, exports, ...others];\n}\n\nfunction sptEntriesOf(binds: StgBinding[]): SptEntry[] {\n  const out: SptEntry[] = [];\n  for (const b of binds) {\n    const pairs: Array<[Binder, StgRhs]> = b.tag === \"StgNonRec\" ? [[b.binder, b.rhs]] : b.pairs;\n    for (const [binder] of pairs) {\n      if (binder.sptFingerprint) out.push({ id: binder, fingerprint: binder.sptFingerprint });\n    }\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** Generate the global unit that all other blocks in the module depend on;\n * used for cost centres and static initializers. The global unit has no\n * dependencies and exports the moduleGlobalSymbol. */\nfunction generateGlobalBlock(gs: GenState, m: Module, sptEntries: SptEntry[]): LinkableUnit {\n  const staticInit = initStaticPtrs(gs, sptEntries);\n  const stat = mconcatJStgStat([...gs.gsGlobal].reverse(), staticInit);\n  const syms = [moduleGlobalSymbol(m)];\n  return {\n    luObjBlock: { oiSymbols: syms, oiClInfo: [], oiStatic: [], oiStat: stat },\n    luIdExports: [],\n    luOtherExports: syms,\n    luIdDeps: [],\n    luDepSymbols: [],\n    luRequired: sptEntries.length > 0,\n  };\n}"}

{"path":"/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/expr.ts","streamContent":"/** GHC's `isStrictId`: join points are never treated as evaluated; otherwise\n * a function closure, a post-case binder, or an explicit strict bit. */\nfunction isStrictId(i: Binder): boolean {\n  if (i.join) return false;\n  return i.strict === true || i.evaluated === true || (i.funRepArity ?? 0) > 0;\n}\n\n/** Unlifted / void / prim-suffixed names stand in for `isStrictType`. */\nfunction isStrictTypeId(i: Binder): boolean {\n  return i.arity === 0 || i.name.endsWith(\"#\");\n}\n\nfunction isUnboxedTupleId(i: Binder): boolean {\n  return i.name.startsWith(\"(#\");\n}\n\nfunction isLocalId(i: Binder): boolean {\n  return i.exported !== true || i.internal === true;\n}\n\n/** GHC.StgToJS.Apply.jumpToII - avoid one indirection for global ids. */\nfunction jumpToII(gs: GenState, i: Binder, vars: JStgExpr[], loadAppInR1: JStgStat): JStgStat {\n  const loadArgs = assignAllReverseOrder([...regsFromR1.slice(1).map(register)], vars);\n  if (isLocalId(i)) {\n    return mconcatJStgStat([loadArgs, loadAppInR1, returnS(closureInfo(varForId(gs, i)))]);\n  }\n  return mconcatJStgStat([loadArgs, loadAppInR1, returnS(varForEntryId(gs, i))]);\n}\n\n/** GHC.StgToJS.Apply.genApp - generate an application of some args to an Id.\n *\n * Case order follows Apply.hs. Binder facts (`funRepArity`, `strict`,\n * `evaluated`, `mightBeFun`) stand in for the Core `Id` queries GHC makes. */\nexport function genApp(gs: GenState, ctx: ExprCtx, i: Binder, args: StgArg[]): [JStgStat, ExprResult] {\n  const st = stackState(gs);\n  const ghc = getSettings(gs).optimization !== \"none\";\n\n  // let-no-escape\n  const n = ctxLneBindingStackSize(ctx, i);\n  if (n !== undefined) {\n    const as_ = args.flatMap((a) => genArg(gs, a));\n    const ei = varForEntryId(gs, i);\n    const ra = mconcatJStgStat(as_.map((a, k) => assign_(register(regsFromR1[k]), a)).reverse());\n    const p = pushLneFrame(gs, n, ctx);\n    const a = adjSp(st, 1);\n    return [mconcatJStgStat([ra, p, a, returnS(ei)]), \"ExprCont\"];\n  }\n\n  // proxy#\n  if (args.length === 0 && i.name === \"proxy#\") {\n    const tops = ctxTarget(ctx).flatMap((t) => t.expr);\n    if (tops.length === 1) return [assign_(tops[0], null_), \"ExprInline\"];\n  }\n\n  const targets = ctxTarget(ctx);\n\n  // unboxed tuple or strict type: return fields individually\n  if (args.length === 0 && targets.length > 0 && (isUnboxedTupleId(i) || isStrictTypeId(i))) {\n    return [storeIdFields(gs, i, targets), \"ExprInline\"];\n  }\n\n  // unboxable heap object already evaluated: Int/Double payload, or the box\n  const vt = idJSRep(i);\n  if (args.length === 0 && vt.length === 1 && isUnboxable(vt[0]!) && ctxIsEvaluated(i)) {\n    const fields = targets.flatMap((t) => t.expr);\n    const is = varsForId(gs, i);\n    if (fields.length === 1 && is.length === 1) {\n      return [assign_(fields[0]!, if_(isObject(is[0]!), closureField1(is[0]!), is[0]!)), \"ExprInline\"];\n    }\n  }\n\n  // known evaluated (or strict type): return fields without entering\n  if (args.length === 0 && targets.length > 0 && (ctxIsEvaluated(i) || isStrictTypeId(i))) {\n    return [storeIdFields(gs, i, targets), \"ExprInline\"];\n  }\n\n  // no args and Id can't be a function: just enter it\n  if (args.length === 0 && i.mightBeFun === false) {\n    const enterId = genIdArg(gs, i);\n    if (enterId.length === 1) return [returnS(app(hdEntryStr, [enterId[0]!])), \"ExprCont\"];\n  }\n\n  const arity = i.funRepArity;\n  const loadFun = assignAll([...regsFromR1.map(register)], varsForId(gs, i));\n\n  // fully saturated global function\n  if (ghc && args.length !== 0 && arity === args.length && !isLocalId(i) && isStrictId(i)) {\n    const vars = args.flatMap((a) => genArg(gs, a));\n    return [jumpToII(gs, i, vars, loadFun), \"ExprCont\"];\n  }\n\n  // oversaturated function: push extra args as a continuation, then jump\n  if (ghc && arity !== undefined && arity < args.length && isStrictId(i) && arity > 0) {\n    const reg = args.slice(0, arity);\n return [jumpToFast(gs, args, loadFun), \"ExprCont\"];\n}"}

{"path":"/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/codegen.ts","streamContent":"function generateBlock(gs: GenState, topBind: StgBinding, globalSym: string): LinkableUnit | undefined {\n  const tl = genToplevel(gs, topBind);\n  const extraTl = gs.gsGroup.ggsToplevelStats;\n  const ci = gs.gsGroup.ggsClosureInfo;\n  const si = gs.gsGroup.ggsStatic;\n  const unf = gs.gsUnfloated;\n  resetGroup(gs);\n\n  const allDeps = collectIds(unf, topBind);\n  const topDeps = collectTopIds(topBind);\n  const required = hasExport(topBind);\n  const stat = mconcatJStgStat([...[...extraTl].reverse(), tl]);\n  const syms = topDeps.map((i) => identForId(gs, i));\n  return {\n    luObjBlock: { oiSymbols: syms, oiClInfo: [...ci].reverse(), oiStatic: [...si].reverse(), oiStat: stat },\n    luIdExports: topDeps,\n    luOtherExports: [],\n    luIdDeps: allDeps,\n    luDepSymbols: [...allDeps.map((i) => identForId(gs, i)), globalSym],\n    luRequired: required,\n  };\n}"}

[]), ...desugared.diagnostics], []);\n  const stg = coreToStg(desugared.program);\n  return stgToJS(defaultStgToJSConfig, { unitId: 'main', moduleName: 'Main' }, stg.bindings);\n}\n\ntest('GHC mode /return h\\$ap_2_2_fast\\(\\);/);\n});\n\ntest('GHC mode pushes a continuation then jumps on oversaturated known-arity calls', () => {\n  const js = renderModule(\n    defaultStgToJSConfig,\n ## Assistant



The crash is `sptRegisterStep` unpacking a thunk: `case p of` reuses `p` as the case binder, then marking it `evaluated` skips the enter. I'll stop treating those binders as already WHNF.

{"path":"/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/expr-ctx.ts","streamContent":"/** Predicate: do we know for sure that the given Id is evaluated?\n *\n * GHC answers this from the tag signature InferTags attached to the Id\n * (`idTagSig_maybe`, plus a JoinId veto on `idDetails`). ghcts records the\n * same fact on `Binder.evaluated` after a `case` whose binder is a *fresh*\n * name for the WHNF. `Binder.strict` is `isStrictId` (function closures are\n * not thunks) and must not be read here: that would make `isInlineApp`\n * skip entering a data thunk. */\nexport function ctxIsEvaluated(i: Binder): boolean {\n  if (i.join) return false;\n  return i.evaluated === true;\n}"}

```

{"path":"/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/core-to-stg.ts","streamContent":"        const altType = altTypeOf(e.alts, e.scrutinee);\n        // The case binder names WHNF only when it is not the scrutinee\n        // itself. Desugar reuses a `Var` scrutinee as the binder (`case p of\n        // p { DEFAULT -> ... }`) so the Case still enters; marking that\n        // binder evaluated makes `isInlineApp` skip the enter and unpack a\n        // thunk.\n        if (scrutVar === undefined || scrutVar.unique !== e.binder.unique)\n          e.binder.evaluated = true;"}

## Assistant



GHC 9.12.2’s event/SPT/apply contract is now in the backend, and the Lynx counter responds on the first tap.



**What changed**



- **SPT:** `initStaticPtrs` is emitted on the module global block, so `h$runInitStatic` fills the table on both Lynx threads before `main`. `sptRegisterThen` stays as a second, idempotent insert.

- **Callbacks / JSVal:** `JSVal` is emitted under GHC’s z-name so `MK_JSVAL` matches. Async and sync callbacks use `h$makeCallback` / `h$makeCallbackApply`.

- **`genApp`:** Function binders get `funRepArity` and `strict`; case binders get `evaluated` only when they are a *fresh* WHNF name. Saturated calls jump to the entry; oversaturated calls `pushCont` then jump.

- **Crash fix:** Desugar’s `case p of` reuses `p` as the case binder. Marking that binder evaluated made codegen unpack thunks (including `sptRegisterStep`) without entering. That bit is no longer set when binder and scrutinee are the same Id, and `ctxIsEvaluated` no longer treats `strict` as “fields are readable.”



**Verified**



- All 71 unit tests pass, including SPT init, JSVal z-name, and saturated/oversat jumps.

- `lynx-run --tap 3` goes **0 → 1** on the first dispatch; `--tap 9` goes **0 → -1**.

- Lynx Explorer was reloaded with `http://localhost:8080/counter/main.lynx.bundle` (A/B still at `/counter-ghc/main.lynx.bundle`).

- Miso still has only the two payload fixes: `osVersion :: Maybe` and recursive `eventJSON`. No extra compiler workarounds.



**Left on purpose**



Demand/WorkWrap was not widened. After the apply path is GHC-shaped, first-try taps work. The remaining gap vs the oracle is mostly marshalling (`fromJSVal` 651 vs 23) and extra closure sites — that is simplifier inlining, not a third calling convention. Runtime-report now shows **2620 direct entry returns** (previously 0); specialized `h$ap_*_fast` dropped because those calls jump to the entry instead.

## Assistant



The reels tab fails in MTS decode because `layoutChangeDetailDecoder` requires `detail.id`, but Lynx Explorer (especially Android) often sends geometry in `params` and an empty or id-less `detail`. That is a payload/decoder mismatch, not a missing compiler pass. I'll make the decoder accept that shape and copy `detail`/`params` even when they are host objects.

{"path":"/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/references/miso/src/Miso/Native/Element/View/Event.hs","streamContent":"layoutChangeDetailDecoder :: Decoder LayoutChangeDetailEvent\nlayoutChangeDetailDecoder = Decoder {..}\n  where\n    decodeAt = DecodeTarget mempty\n    decoder = withObject \"LayoutChangeDetailEvent\" $ \\o -> do\n      -- Lynx types put the box on `detail` (with `id` and `dataset`). Released\n      -- Android engines still fill the deprecated `params` object instead, and\n      -- omit `id` when the node has no id selector — the reels track is one of\n      -- those. Union the two and treat id/dataset as optional so MTS layout\n      -- still reaches SwipeInit / ReelsLayout.\n      d <- M.union <$> (o .:? \"detail\" .!= M.empty) <*> (o .:? \"params\" .!= M.empty)\n      LayoutChangeDetailEvent\n        <$> d .:? \"id\" .!= \"\"\n        <*> d .:? \"width\" .!= 0\n        <*> d .:? \"height\" .!= 0\n        <*> d .:? \"top\" .!= 0\n        <*> d .:? \"right\" .!= 0\n        <*> d .:? \"bottom\" .!= 0\n        <*> d .:? \"left\" .!= 0\n        <*> d .:? \"dataset\" .!= M.empty"}

{"path":"/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/references/miso/ts/miso/event.ts","streamContent":"  for (const a of at) obj = obj[a];\n  /* If obj is a list-like object */\n  var newObj;\n  if (isArrayLike(obj)) {\n    newObj = [];\n    for (var j = 0; j < obj.length; j++) {\n      newObj.push(eventJSON([], obj[j], depth + 1));\n    }\n    return newObj;\n  }\n  /* If obj is a non-list-like object */\n  newObj = {};\n  for (var key in getAllPropertyNames(obj)) {\n    /* bug in safari, throws TypeError if the following fields are referenced on a checkbox */\n    /* https://stackoverflow.com/a/25569117/453261 */\n    /* https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/input.html#do-not-apply */\n    if (\n      obj['localName'] === 'input' &&\n      (key === 'selectionDirection' || key === 'selectionStart' || key === 'selectionEnd')\n    ) {\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (\n      typeof obj[key] == 'string' ||\n      typeof obj[key] == 'number' ||\n      typeof obj[key] == 'boolean'\n    ) {\n      newObj[key] = obj[key];\n    } else if (depth < 4 && shouldCopyNested(key, obj[key])) {\n      /* Lynx custom events (`layout` / `layoutchange`, animation, appear)\n         put the payload on nested `detail` / `params`. Recurse into those\n         even when they are host objects, and into other plain objects, but\n         skip DOM nodes (`target`, `currentTarget`). */\n      newObj[key] = eventJSON([], obj[key], depth + 1);\n    }\n  }\n  return newObj;\n}\n\nfunction isArrayLike(obj: any): boolean {\n  if (obj == null) return false;\n  if (obj instanceof Array) return true;\n  if (typeof obj !== 'object') return false;\n  if (obj['localName'] === 'select') return false;\n  if (typeof obj.length !== 'number') return false;\n  return obj.length === 0 || 0 in obj;\n}\n\n/* A payload object, as opposed to a DOM node or any other host object: its\n   prototype is Object.prototype, or it has none at all. */\nfunction isPlainObject(v: any): boolean {\n  if (v === null || typeof v !== 'object') return false;\n  const p = Object.getPrototypeOf(v);\n  return p === Object.prototype || p === null;\n}\n\nfunction shouldCopyNested(key: string, v: any): boolean {\n  if (isPlainObject(v)) return true;\n  return (key === 'detail' || key === 'params') && v !== null && typeof v === 'object';\n}"}

{"path":"/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/references/miso/ts/spec/eventJSON.spec.ts","streamContent":"  test('copies nested detail.id for Lynx layoutchange', () => {\n    const ev = {\n      type: 'layoutchange',\n      detail: {\n        id: 'box',\n        width: 10,\n        height: 20,\n        top: 0,\n        right: 10,\n        bottom: 20,\n        left: 0,\n        dataset: { k: 'v' },\n      },\n    };\n    const res: any = eventJSON([], ev);\n    expect(res.detail.id).toBe('box');\n    expect(res.detail.height).toBe(20);\n    expect(res.detail.dataset).toEqual({ k: 'v' });\n  });\n\n  test('copies Android params when detail is empty', () => {\n    const ev = {\n      type: 'layout',\n      detail: {},\n      params: { width: 390, height: 844, left: 0, top: 0, right: 390, bottom: 844 },\n    };\n    const res: any = eventJSON([], ev);\n    expect(res.detail).toEqual({});\n    expect(res.params).toEqual({\n      width: 390,\n      height: 844,\n      left: 0,\n      top: 0,\n      right: 390,\n      bottom: 844,\n    });\n  });\n\n  test('does not treat a length-bearing payload as a list unless it has indices', () => {\n    const ev = { type: 'layout', length: 390, detail: { width: 390, height: 844 } };\n    const res: any = eventJSON([], ev);\n    expect(Array.isArray(res)).toBeFalse();\n    expect(res.length).toBe(390);\n    expect(res.detail.width).toBe(390);\n  });\n\n  test('accepts nested at arrays ([[path]]) and returns tuple of results', () => {"}

## Assistant



The reels crash is a **Lynx layout payload mismatch**, not a missing compiler pass.



`[MTS dispatch] decode error: Key not found: id` is `layoutChangeDetailDecoder` failing on the reels swiper. That screen wires MTS `layout` / `layoutchange` on every `track` (and BTS layout on the outer reels view) so `SwipeInit` and `ReelsLayout` can run. The decoder did `detail.id` as required. Lynx’s types list `id` there, but Explorer — especially Android — often sends:



- geometry on the deprecated **`params`** object (`width` / `height` / box), not `detail`

- a **`detail` that is `{}` or has no `id`**, because the track views never set an `id` selector



Decode got as far as `detail` (that’s why the error is `id`, not `detail`) and then died. `SwipeInit` never ran, so the vertical pager stayed inert.



Taps still worked because they don’t use this decoder. Home-feed carousels use the same `track` handlers, so they can log the same error without looking as broken.



**What changed**



- Decoder unions `detail` with `params`, and treats `id` / `dataset` / box fields as optional (same idea as `osVersion` / `attachedCells`).

- `eventJSON` still copies nested `detail` / `params`, including host objects, and no longer treats a random `length` field as an array.



Misogram’s Lynx bundle is rebuilt. Reload Explorer on the existing host (`:8080`) and open Reels — that decode error should be gone and vertical swipe should initialize.



Throttles are separate: the event/frame path still allocates more closures and runs `fromJSVal` far more than GHC. This fix does not address that.



## User



Swipe is realtime, the dots to track the images swiped is on is severly delayed adn taps are 2-5 seconds slow, page transition are non smooth but are quick



## Assistant



Swipe staying realtime while dots and taps lag points at the background-thread update path, not the main-thread gesture handlers. I’ll trace how dots, taps, and page transitions get from event to render and compare that with what GHC does.

## Assistant



Swipe paint was already realtime on the main thread. The dots, taps, and transitions were waiting on the background thread to **encode the whole model as JSON** on every commit. That encoder was walking Generic dictionaries, which is enough work to stall Lynx’s JS thread for seconds.



`deriving anyclass (ToJSON, FromJSON)` now generates a direct `object` / `withObject` spine (same JSON shape as aeson defaults: records as `{"field": …}`, all-nullary sums as `"Home"`, mixed sums as `{"tag":"SetTab","contents":…}`). Hydrate no longer goes through `from` / `Rep`.



The misogram bundle is rebuilt at `references/misogram/dist/lynx/main.lynx.bundle`. Reload Explorer (the host on `:8080` will pick up the new file).



Please check on device:



1. **Carousel dots** — they should move when the snap settles, not seconds later.

2. **Like / tab taps** — should feel like a normal tap, not a 2–5s pause.

3. **Push/pop** — still a short animation; it should hitch less because the JS thread is no longer in a long Generic walk.



I could not tap the device from here, so this is not confirmed end-to-end yet. If taps are still slow after reload, the remaining cost is the full view rebuild, not JSON.

</user_visible_high_level_summary>

<response>

**Highest-leverage remaining gap: Specialise + INLINE + WorkWrap for `FromJSVal` / event decode** — still **651 vs 23** decoder refs and **~2× closures** after Phase 1–3. That is what still blows the ~30ms JS long-task budget on every tap.



Live `runtime-report` (counter, still current):



| metric | ghcts | GHC 9.12.2 oracle |

|---|---:|---:|

| closure alloc sites | **8838** | **4868** (~1.8×) |

| `fromJSVal*` refs | **651** | **23** (~28×) |

| `$w` worker symbols | **0** | hundreds (`$wdispatchEvent`, `$wbuildVTree`, `$w$cfromJSValUnchecked`, …) |

| specialized applies | 1080 | 3468 |

| generic applies | 7 | 292 |



No saved report notes beyond `scripts/runtime-report.mjs` itself; numbers above are from running it on `references/miso-lynx/examples/dist/jsexe/all.js` vs `oracle-jsexe/all.js`.



---



### Ranked remaining GHC parity gaps (tap/event latency)



1. **Missing Specialise / real class-method specialization for marshalling (highest leverage)** 

 - **Files:** `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/elaborate.ts` (`specializeMethod` ~1512), `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/core-opt/inline.ts`, `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/lexer.ts` (pragma skip ~99) 

 - **Why taps:** `Miso.Event.onWithOptions` does `fromJSVal` / `fromJSValUnchecked` on every event (`Maybe Bool`, `Maybe Int`, `Value`, …). GHC turns those into inlined/`$w$cfromJSValUnchecked` workers; ghcts keeps hundreds of `$cFromJSVal*` / `$dmFromJSVal*` call sites. 

 - **`specializeMethod` limits:** needs `dict.tag === "var"`, `$fClass…` name, **empty instance context**, and **refuses default methods** (`$dm…`). Constrained/`Maybe`/`[]` defaults stay on the dict path. 

 - **INLINE ignored:** lexer discards `{-# … #-}`; Miso’s `{-# INLINE fromJSVal #-}` never reaches the pipeline.



2. **No WorkWrap / worker–wrapper (Phase 4 cancelled — still the big post-tap cost)** 

 - **Files:** absent; only `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/core-opt/strictness.ts` `forceStrictArgs` (demand → `case`, **no unbox**) 

 - **Why taps:** GHC emits `$wdispatchEvent`, `$wbuildVTree`, `$w$cfromJSValUnchecked`. ghcts has **zero** `$w*`. That drives the **~2× `h$c*` sites** and keeps Int/`Maybe`/record work boxed through BTS update/diff/vtree after the tap.



3. **Tiny Simplifier (`MAX_BODY = 24`) vs GHC Specialise+Simplifier** 

 - **File:** `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/core-opt/inline.ts` 

 - **Why taps:** Only saturated, trivial-arg, non-recursive, ≤24-node bodies inline. Event decode / `Maybe` / parser combinators stay as separate closures → more alloc + enters on the same BTS slice.



4. **Partial DmdAnal (`forceStrictArgs` only)** 

 - **Files:** `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/core-opt/strictness.ts`, wired in `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/build.ts` ~356–359 

 - **Why taps:** Forces known-strict args with `case`, but no CPR/product demand, no unboxing, weak through unknown callees. Update loops and decode still allocate thunks GHC evaluates once and unwraps.



5. **Incomplete CorePrep in Core→STG** 

 - **File:** `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/core-to-stg.ts` (`floatToTop` ~68–76, `mkClosure` ~167–171, SingleEntry never emitted ~161–166) 

 - **Why taps:** Narrow float (only always-sinkable cons); every 0-arg closure is **Updatable**. Extra thunks on ANF’d event/update args → more alloc/GC under the long-task budget.



6. **InferTags / evaluated facts still thin (genApp mostly done)** 

 - **Files:** `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/expr.ts` `genApp`, `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/expr-ctx.ts` `ctxIsEvaluated`, `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/stg-js/closure.ts` `mkClosure` 

 - **Why taps:** Saturated/oversat jumps work (`test/ghc-js.test.mjs`). Remaining miss: no InferTags → fewer `ExprInline`/field returns; more `jumpToFast`/`h$e` than a fully tagged GHC pipeline. Secondary vs (1)–(2): apply path is no longer the main divergence.



7. **Stock ToJSON/FromJSON for Model ≠ event `FromJSVal`** 

 - **Files:** `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/src/sugar.ts` `derivedJsonSource`; Miso `FromJSVal` in `references/miso/src/Miso/DSL.hs` 

 - **Why taps:** Helps hydrate encode; does **not** shrink the 651 `fromJSVal` event-decode sites. Touch still pays dictionary marshalling before/around BTS work.



---



### Already done (not the remaining gap)

SPT init, JSVal/`h$makeCallback`, `genApp` `funRepArity`/oversat, case-binder `evaluated` crash fix. First-tap correctness is largely fixed; **latency** is still Core-pipeline / specialisation.



### Verdict

Prioritize **(1) Specialise+INLINE for `FromJSVal`/`fromJSValUnchecked` at known types**, then **(2) WorkWrap** on the event/`buildVTree`/`dispatchEvent` path. That is the cancelled Phase 4, but aimed at marshalling — the metric that still diverges by an order of magnitude and sits on every Lynx tap before the model update finishes.

</response>

<task>

kind: subagent

status: success

task_id: 746a3308-4759-42d8-aac8-1ff7669cda45

title: Misogram tap/BTS path

tool_call_id: call-e7af12a6-b372-4be7-83b3-7a78c6bf22e5-153

fc_481b812a-404c-97a7-a5c6-3155cc0f4ab3_0

agent_id: 746a3308-4759-42d8-aac8-1ff7669cda45

detail: <user_visible_high_level_summary>

Stock ToJSON/FromJSON did land in the misogram bundle (object/.=, zero genericToJSON), but taps stay slow because every BTS dirty draw still rebuilds the full VTree, Diff.diff, and MODEL_HYDRATE of the entire Model (including all posts). Compiler divergence vs GHC remains large (~2× closures, ~28× fromJSVal on the counter oracle) and amplifies that path.

</user_visible_high_level_summary>

<response>

## Verdict



Stock JSON derive **did land** and is **not** the remaining tap bottleneck. Taps still feel “big slow” because every BTS UI change still does a **full-tree rebuild + full-Model hydrate**, and GHCTS still pays far more per Haskell step than GHC 9.12.2.



---



### 1. Tap path (misogram)



| Kind | Thread | Path |

|------|--------|------|

| Swipe paint | **MTS only** | `onTouch*MainWith` → `SwipeStart/Move/End` → `swipeStart/Move/End` (`MainThreadRef`) — no BTS round-trip until settle |

| Like / nav / photo tap | **BTS** | plain `VE.onTap` → BTS `updateModel` → dirty → draw + hydrate |



Citations:

- Architecture note: `/Volumes/Storage/Projects/ghcts/references/misogram/src/Main.hs:15-19`

- BTS taps: `ToggleLike` / `TapPhoto` at `Main.hs:131-155`; `VE.onTap` wiring in `View.hs:102-103`, `285-286`, `327`

- MTS swipe: `View.hs:180-202`, `Swiper.hs:8-16`, settle → BTS via `Snapped` → `runOnBG (SetPage …)` at `Main.hs:237-243`

- Double-tap is **two** BTS turns: `TapPhoto` → `io (TapPhotoAt … <$> nowMs)` → second update (`Main.hs:141-155`)



---



### 2. What still runs on BTS after a tap



For any dirty model change (`ToggleLike`, `SetPage`, `Push`, …):



1. Scheduler `commit` applies `update` (`Runtime.hs:440-481`)

2. `renderComponent` → `_componentDraw` **then** `_componentHydrate` (`Runtime.hs:487-490`)

3. Draw: `buildVTree` of **entire** `viewModel` → wait for **rAF** → `Diff.diff` → flush (`Runtime.hs:254-270`)

4. Hydrate (BTS only): `postComponent MODEL_HYDRATE … (Just (toJSON newModel))` (`Runtime.hs:278-280`)

5. MTS receives hydrate and `fromJSON`’s the **whole** model into the replica (`Runtime.hs:2490-2498`) — **no** redraw on MTS



Event ingress on BTS also pays marshalling: `onWithOptions` does `fromJSVal =<< FFI.eventJSON …` then `parseEither` (`Event.hs:205-218`). Cross-thread actions additionally `fromJSON` the `Action` (`Runtime.hs:2325-2356`).



`viewModel` rebuilds the full feed: `map (postCard m) (posts m)` (`View.hs:43-44`, `230-243`) — ~14 posts with captions/comments (`Data.hs:52-109`). A heart toggle still rebuilds every card.



---



### 3. JSON derive: landed in current bundle



| Check | Result |

|-------|--------|

| `genericToJSON` in `jsexe/all.js` / lynx bundle | **0** |

| Model `toJSON` free vars | `MisozuJSONzuobject`, field `ToJSON`s, `.=` path — **not** Generic (`out.js` ~149825) |

| Source | `deriving anyclass (ToJSON, FromJSON)` on `Model` (`Types.hs:120-143`); emitter `derivedJsonSource` → `object [… .= …]` (`sugar.ts:662-719`) |

| Bundle age | `all.js` + `main.lynx.bundle` both **Aug 31 16:47** |



So hydrate no longer walks Generic. It still walks **every field of every post** via stock `object`/list encoders on every dirty draw.



---



### 4. Remaining bottlenecks (ranked by likely tap latency)



**1. Full-Model `MODEL_HYDRATE` every dirty draw (architecture) — highest** 

`Runtime.hs:278-280`, `487-490`, `2490-2498`. Like, dots after swipe (`SetPage`), and nav all ship the entire `Model` (posts + comments + stacks) BTS→MTS as JSON. Stock derive only made this *cheaper per field*; it did not shrink frequency or payload. Explains “dots/taps/page transitions wait on BTS” vs realtime swipe.



**2. Full `buildVTree` + `Diff.diff` (+ rAF wait) on every dirty draw — highest** 

`Runtime.hs:254-270`, `View.hs:230-243`. Heart icon change still rebuilds splash/home/stories/all posts. Draw **blocks on `requestAnimationFrame`** before diff (`262-264`) — adds up to a frame of latency on top of Haskell work.



**3. GHCTS marshalling inflation vs GHC (~28× `fromJSVal`) — very high** 

Counter oracle vs GHCTS (`runtime-report`):



| metric | GHCTS counter | GHC oracle |

|--------|---------------|------------|

| `fromJSVal*` refs | **651** | **23** |

| closure alloc sites | **8711** | **4681** (~1.86×) |



Misogram is worse still: **766** decoder refs, **25952** closure sites (`out.js`). Hits every tap (`eventJSON`/`fromJSVal`), every hydrate (`toJSON`/`fromJSON`), every `postEffect`. Prior notes: remaining gap after SPT/genApp is mostly marshalling + thunks, not apply shape.



**4. Missing full DmdAnal / WorkWrap / simplifier — high** 

Only a narrow force-at-call-site pass (`strictness.ts:1-17`); **no** worker/wrapper unboxing. Inline capped at `MAX_BODY = 24` (`inline.ts:41-43`). Phase 4 was explicitly cancelled. Extra thunks on event decode, JSON list walks, and view construction.



**5. `specializeMethod` gaps — medium-high** 

Refuses default methods, non-`var` dicts, instances with **non-empty context** (`elaborate.ts:1505-1527`). Many `ToJSON`/`Eq`/`Functor` paths stay dictionary-indirect → more enters during hydrate + `dirtyCheck`/`Eq Model`.



**6. Always-ReEntrant / never-SingleEntry + constructor lets — medium** 

`mkClosure` only emits `ReEntrant` or `Updatable`; **SingleEntry deliberately never** (`core-to-stg.ts:151-168`). More update frames / allocations than GHC CorePrep on one-shot lets along the draw/JSON path.



**7. Double-tap / nav extra turns — medium (product)** 

`TapPhoto` → async `nowMs` → `TapPhotoAt` (`Main.hs:141-155`): two scheduler cycles before UI can change. `Push`/`Pop` also `threadDelay` + `runOnMain ResetSwipers` (`Main.hs:116-126`, `250-258`).



**8. Stock Generic JSON — fixed / not remaining** 

Confirmed absent from shipped bundle. Do not re-chase this for tap latency.



---



### 5. Why swipe feels fine but taps don’t



Swipe never touches BTS model ownership until settle (`Swiper.hs:8-16`, `View.hs:196-201`). Taps/dots/transitions **must** go BTS → full view + full hydrate. Compiler divergence multiplies that BTS long-task (Lynx ~30 ms budget; Android watchdog stretches perceived delay).



**Highest-leverage next levers (not edits, just ranking):** shrink/skip hydrate payload (or hydrate only dirty slices); avoid full-tree rebuild for local UI (like/dots); then close GHC marshalling/WorkWrap gap so the remaining BTS work fits the frame budget.

</response>
